{"parentUuid":"fc96c420-6638-4dbf-b44d-b1c5b43a095b","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01ErADzheFJjYDGWbcasH5Gd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01SRS9HonhkZn7TGd378kfsd","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\auto-reboot-handoff-verify-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh (Phase 3.5 T7-followup; S73).\n#\n# Hook purpose (HH-H.4 Stop hook; defense-in-depth outer fence to HH-H.1's\n# 5-min strict guard inside session-self-reboot.sh):\n#   - reads agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens (TOTAL token count)\n#   - if TOTAL < STOCKFORGE_WIND_DOWN_TOKENS (default 180000) → exit 0 (no-op)\n#   - else: checks agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md mtime\n#   - if checkpoint age <= 7200s (2h) → exit 0 + clear any prior pre-block\n#     marker so budget-watchdog can fire normally\n#   - else: writes .auto-reboot-PRE-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint marker AND\n#     appends URGENT entry to human-workspace/notifications/urgent.md\n#   - always exits 0 (soft-warn never blocks Stop chain)\n#\n# 5 test cases:\n#   TC1 — no token file → silent no-op (no marker, no urgent)\n#   TC2 — tokens below wind_down → silent no-op (no marker, no urgent)\n#   TC3 — tokens >= wind_down + fresh checkpoint (<2h) → no marker, no urgent\n#   TC4 — tokens >= wind_down + stale checkpoint (>2h) → marker exists +\n#         URGENT line appended\n#   TC5 — tokens >= wind_down + missing checkpoint → marker exists + URGENT\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nMARKER=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.auto-reboot-PRE-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint\"\nURGENT=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/notifications/urgent.md\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTOKEN_FILE=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens\"\nCHECKPOINT=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints\" \\\n           \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no token file → silent no-op ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0 (no token file); got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$MARKER\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: pre-block marker should NOT exist when no token file\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$URGENT\" ] && grep -q \"AUTO-REBOOT BLOCKED\" \"$URGENT\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: URGENT should not contain BLOCKED line when no token file\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no token file → silent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC2: tokens below wind_down → silent no-op ---\nclean_state\necho \"100000\" > \"$TOKEN_FILE\"\necho \"# Checkpoint clean\" > \"$CHECKPOINT\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; {"parentUuid":"a8a79a5b-738a-4dad-9f03-21304616c7c0","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_0176NQfL32NmAmJjnyQqJNAE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VHcr4uXu1QxRrvAnEziYyV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\subagent-stop-logger-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for subagent-stop-logger.sh (Phase 3.5 T7-followup; S73).\n#\n# Hook purpose (HH-B.2-related; SubagentStop hook):\n#   - reads stdin JSON {agent_id, status, session_id} via jq if available,\n#     else regex fallback\n#   - falls back to env vars CLAUDE_AGENT_ID / CLAUDE_AGENT_STATUS /\n#     CLAUDE_SESSION_ID when stdin fields empty\n#   - if BOTH agent_id AND status empty after fallback → stderr skip msg + exit 0\n#   - else: appends one structured line to .session-hooks.log:\n#         \"[<TS>] SubagentStop agentId=<a> status=<s> session=<sid>\"\n#   - if BOTH agent_id AND session_id present → also appends marker line to\n#     observations/_subagent-stops/<UTC-date>.log\n#   - always exits 0\n#\n# 5 test cases:\n#   TC1 — no stdin + no env → skip with stderr msg; no log file\n#   TC2 — stdin JSON (all 3 fields) → log line + observation file\n#   TC3 — env vars only → log line written; observation if both ids present\n#   TC4 — stdin partial (status only, no agent_id) but env empty → log line\n#         written (status != empty satisfies \"agent_id OR status\" guard);\n#         observation NOT written (agent_id empty)\n#   TC5 — malformed stdin (not JSON) + env populated → falls back to env;\n#         log line written\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../subagent-stop-logger.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nOBS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations/_subagent-stops\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n  unset CLAUDE_AGENT_ID CLAUDE_AGENT_STATUS CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no stdin + no env → skip with stderr msg ---\nclean_state\nSTDERR=\"$TEMPDIR/tc1.stderr\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>\"$STDERR\" || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  cat \"$STDERR\" || true\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"no agent_id or status available\" \"$STDERR\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected skip msg in stderr\"\n  cat \"$STDERR\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: log should NOT be written when both agent_id+status empty\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no stdin + no env → skip with stderr msg\"\n\n# --- TC2: stdin JSON (all 3 fields) → log line + observation file ---\nclean_state\nSTDIN_JSON='{\"agent_id\":\"sandwich-dev\",\"status\":\"completed\",\"session_id\":\"S73-test\"}'\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<<\"$STDIN_JSON\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected log file at $LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"agentId=sandwich-dev\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: log should contain 'agentId=sandwich-dev'\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"status=completed\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: log should contain 'status=completed'\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"session=S73-test\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: log should contain 'session=S73-test'\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# Observation file: _subagent-stops/<UTC-date>.log should exist\nOBS_FILES=$(find \"$OBS_DIR\" -name \"*.log\" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\nif [ \"$OBS_FILES\" -lt 1 ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected ≥1 observation log file in $OBS_DIR\"\n  ls -la \"$OBS_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -rq \"agent_id=sandwich-dev\" \"$OBS_DIR\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: observation log should record agent_id=sandwich-dev\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: stdin JSON full → log line + observation file\"\n\n# --- TC3: env vars only → log line written ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_AGENT_ID=\"env-agent\" \\\n  CLAUDE_AGENT_STATUS=\"ok\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"env-session\" \\\n  bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"agentId=env-agent\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: env-fallback agentId should appear in log\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"status=ok\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: env-fallback status should appear in log\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: env vars only → log line written\"\n\n# --- TC4: stdin partial (status only) + env empty → log line, no observation ---\nclean_state\nSTDIN_JSON='{\"status\":\"failed\"}'\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<<\"$STDIN_JSON\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: log should be written when status alone is non-empty\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"status=failed\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: log should contain 'status=failed'\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# Observation file should NOT exist (agent_id and session_id both empty).\nif [ -d \"$OBS_DIR\" ] && [ \"$(find \"$OBS_DIR\" -name \"*.log\" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: observation log should NOT be written when agent_id is empty\"\n  ls -la \"$OBS_DIR\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: stdin partial → log line, no observation (agent_id empty)\"\n\n# --- TC5: malformed stdin + env populated → falls back to env ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_AGENT_ID=\"fallback-agent\" \\\n  CLAUDE_AGENT_STATUS=\"ok\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"fallback-session\" \\\n  bash \"$HOOK\" <<<\"this is not json\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected exit 0 (graceful fallback); got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! {"parentUuid":"1b904d48-4cc1-4459-a8bc-4bf6b93dbb4d","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01SAyfHjovaYcXJyReKvqiHz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01X8vWqo28Rhxqvz1Nn4KmAV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\vendor-api-probe-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for vendor-api-probe.sh (Phase 3.5 T7-followup; S73).\n#\n# Hook purpose (HH-A.1; SessionStart hook; per L-S28-1 + L-S32-1):\n#   - reads agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md to find active plan path\n#     (matches `session-plans/pending/<digits>...md`)\n#   - if no current-execution.md OR no plan ref OR plan file missing → exit 0\n#   - else: for each lib in whitelist (vnstock yfinance httpx anthropic openai\n#     duckdb pgvector), grep plan for `import <lib>|from <lib>|<lib>.`; if hit,\n#     run python -c \"importlib.import_module('<lib>')\" + log result to\n#     .vendor-api-probe.log\n#   - additionally counts multi-strategy ladder hits (Strategy A[1-4] /\n#     alternative [A-D]: / ladder regex); if count >= 3 → log + stderr advisory\n#   - always exits 0 (advisory only)\n#\n# 5 test cases (chooses libs by local-environment availability):\n#   TC1 — no current-execution.md → exit 0; no log\n#   TC2 — current-execution.md but no plan path → exit 0; no log\n#   TC3 — plan with whitelist lib (httpx, available locally) → probe log entry\n#   TC4 — plan with whitelist lib (anthropic, not installed locally) → FAIL log\n#         entry + stderr \"vendor-api-probe FAIL\"\n#   TC5 — plan with ≥3 multi-strategy ladder markers → log \"multi-strategy\n#         ladder detected\" + stderr advisory\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../vendor-api-probe.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nEXEC_FILE=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.vendor-api-probe.log\"\nPLAN_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/session-plans/pending\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$PLAN_DIR\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no current-execution.md → exit 0, no log ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: log should not exist when no current-execution.md\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no current-execution.md → silent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC2: current-execution.md but no plan ref → exit 0, no log ---\nclean_state\necho \"# Current execution — no active plan referenced\" > \"$EXEC_FILE\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: log should not exist when current-execution has no plan path\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: current-execution.md without plan ref → silent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC3: plan with whitelist lib (httpx, locally importable) → probe log entry ---\nclean_state\nPLAN_PATH=\"agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/099-test-vendor-import.md\"\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$TEMPDIR/$PLAN_PATH\")\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/$PLAN_PATH\" <<'EOF'\n# Test plan\nThis plan does:\nimport httpx\nEOF\necho \"Active plan: $PLAN_PATH\" > \"$EXEC_FILE\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected probe log at $LOG\"\n  ls -la \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/\" 2>/dev/null\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"probe httpx\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: log should contain 'probe httpx' entry\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: plan with import httpx → probe log entry\"\n\n# --- TC4: plan with non-installed whitelist lib (anthropic) → FAIL log + stderr ---\nclean_state\nPLAN_PATH=\"agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/099-test-vendor-fail.md\"\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$TEMPDIR/$PLAN_PATH\")\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/$PLAN_PATH\" <<'EOF'\n# Test plan\nThis plan does:\nimport anthropic\nEOF\necho \"Active plan: $PLAN_PATH\" > \"$EXEC_FILE\"\nSTDERR=\"$TEMPDIR/tc4.stderr\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>\"$STDERR\" || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; Production layout has\n# plans under agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/; the path-prefix\n# mismatch is a latent observation surfaced via S73 audit (potential\n# follow-up: hook regex should match `(agent-workspace/)?session-plans/...`).\nPLAN_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/session-plans/pending\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/session-plans\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$PLAN_DIR\"\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":395,"cache_read_input_tokens":174018,"output_tokens":4604,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":395,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":4604,"cache_read_input_tokens":174018,"cache_creation_input_tokens":395,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":395},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CakhxoPuD5kVb4TUj2Ssz","type":"assistant","uuid":"301197f3-6f5a-4063-aa9d-8b4912c16496","timestamp":"2026-05-06T04:45:47.412Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"554b9306-d1ad-4754-8f04-73011ef8a3a4","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"301197f3-6f5a-4063-aa9d-8b4912c16496","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"757fc6fc-926c-4b6e-9f51-6e56663f5d14","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DNG86ksEzschhqoGfmWpez","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\vendor-api-probe-fire-test.sh has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"98a9b73b-e203-4664-889c-5031e97c20e8","timestamp":"2026-05-06T04:45:47.957Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\vendor-api-probe-fire-test.sh","oldString":"EXEC_FILE=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.vendor-api-probe.log\"\nPLAN_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/session-plans/pending\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$PLAN_DIR\"\n}","newString":"EXEC_FILE=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.vendor-api-probe.log\"\n# Note: the hook's regex `session-plans/pending/...` resolves relative to\n# $PROJECT_DIR (without `agent-workspace/` prefix), so test fixtures stage\n# plans at $TEMPDIR/session-plans/pending/<file>.md. Production layout has\n# plans under agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/; the path-prefix\n# mismatch is a latent observation surfaced via S73 audit (potential\n# follow-up: hook regex should match `(agent-workspace/)?session-plans/...`).\nPLAN_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/session-plans/pending\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/session-plans\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$PLAN_DIR\"\n}","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for vendor-api-probe.sh (Phase 3.5 T7-followup; S73).\n#\n# Hook purpose (HH-A.1; SessionStart hook; per L-S28-1 + L-S32-1):\n#   - reads agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md to find active plan path\n#     (matches `session-plans/pending/<digits>...md`)\n#   - if no current-execution.md OR no plan ref OR plan file missing → exit 0\n#   - else: for each lib in whitelist (vnstock yfinance httpx anthropic openai\n#     duckdb pgvector), grep plan for `import <lib>|from <lib>|<lib>.`; if hit,\n#     run python -c \"importlib.import_module('<lib>')\" + log result to\n#     .vendor-api-probe.log\n#   - additionally counts multi-strategy ladder hits (Strategy A[1-4] /\n#     alternative [A-D]: / ladder regex); if count >= 3 → log + stderr advisory\n#   - always exits 0 (advisory only)\n#\n# 5 test cases (chooses libs by local-environment availability):\n#   TC1 — no current-execution.md → exit 0; no log\n#   TC2 — current-execution.md but no plan path → exit 0; no log\n#   TC3 — plan with whitelist lib (httpx, available locally) → probe log entry\n#   TC4 — plan with whitelist lib (anthropic, not installed locally) → FAIL log\n#         entry + stderr \"vendor-api-probe FAIL\"\n#   TC5 — plan with ≥3 multi-strategy ladder markers → log \"multi-strategy\n#         ladder detected\" + stderr advisory\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../vendor-api-probe.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nEXEC_FILE=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.vendor-api-probe.log\"\nPLAN_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/session-plans/pending\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$PLAN_DIR\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no current-execution.md → exit 0, no log ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: log should not exist when no current-execution.md\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no current-execution.md → silent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC2: current-execution.md but no plan ref → exit 0, no log ---\nclean_state\necho \"# Current execution — no active plan referenced\" > \"$EXEC_FILE\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: log should not exist when current-execution has no plan path\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: current-execution.md without plan ref → silent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC3: plan with whitelist lib (httpx, locally importable) → probe log entry ---\nclean_state\nPLAN_PATH=\"agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/099-test-vendor-import.md\"\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$TEMPDIR/$PLAN_PATH\")\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/$PLAN_PATH\" <<'EOF'\n# Test plan\nThis plan does:\nimport httpx\nEOF\necho \"Active plan: $PLAN_PATH\" > \"$EXEC_FILE\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../vendor-api-probe.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nEXEC_FILE=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.vendor-api-probe.log\"\n# Note: the hook's regex `session-plans/pending/...` resolves relative to\n# $PROJECT_DIR (without `agent-workspace/` prefix), so test fixtures stage\n# plans at $TEMPDIR/session-plans/pending/<file>.md. Production layout has\n# plans under agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/; the path-prefix\n# mismatch is a latent observation surfaced via S73 audit (potential\n# follow-up: hook regex should match `(agent-workspace/)?session-plans/...`).\nPLAN_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/session-plans/pending\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/session-plans\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$PLAN_DIR\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no current-execution.md → exit 0, no log ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: log should not exist when no current-execution.md\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: no current-execution.md → silent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC2: current-execution.md but no plan ref → exit 0, no log ---\nclean_state\necho \"# Current execution — no active plan referenced\" > \"$EXEC_FILE\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: log should not exist when current-execution has no plan path\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: current-execution.md without plan ref → silent no-op\"\n\n# --- TC3: plan with whitelist lib (httpx, locally importable) → probe log entry ---\nclean_state\nPLAN_PATH=\"agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/099-test-vendor-import.md\"\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$TEMPDIR/$PLAN_PATH\")\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/$PLAN_PATH\" <<'EOF'\n# Test plan\nThis plan does:\nimport httpx\nEOF\necho \"Active plan: $PLAN_PATH\" > \"$EXEC_FILE\"\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected exit 0; got $RC\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! {"parentUuid":"dc008965-8979-4313-87c2-c3bdf1b64337","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_011GvmWhBgdqUBbzEHNEoAz8","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018M4TcJUx1Xm6evNCZYNPiN","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\profile-template-auto-populate-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for profile-template-auto-populate.sh (Phase 3.5 T7-followup; S73).\n#\n# Hook purpose (HH-C.3 Stop hook):\n#   - finds latest session log in agent-workspace/memory/sessions/ within\n#     last 4h (find -mmin -240); if none → exit 0\n#   - extracts task_class from `type:` (or `**Type**:`) frontmatter line\n#   - extracts model from `agent:` line; maps claude-opus-4-7 → opus47-max etc;\n#     defaults to opus47-max\n#   - derives SESSION_N from filename (YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md → SN)\n#   - writes idempotency marker .profile-template-fired-<session-basename>\n#   - if profile card exists at self-awareness/profiles/<model>-<task>.md →\n#     appends SESSION_N to sample_sessions array + increments samples_count\n#   - else → creates stub card with frontmatter + skeleton body\n#   - if samples_count >= STOCKFORGE_PROFILE_REBUILD_THRESHOLD (default 10),\n#     enqueue profile-render ETL job (skipped if MEMORY_ETL_DISABLE=1)\n#\n# 5 test cases:\n#   TC1 — no sessions dir → exit 0; no card created\n#   TC2 — session log exists but >4h old → exit 0; no card created\n#   TC3 — fresh session with type=FOCUSED_IMPL + agent=claude-opus-4-7 →\n#         creates stub card with sample_sessions=[S99]; marker written\n#   TC4 — fresh session + existing card with sample_sessions=[S95, S96] →\n#         appends S99 → sample_sessions=[S95, S96, S99]; samples_count=3\n#   TC5 — idempotency: marker pre-exists → second invocation no-op (card\n#         unchanged)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../profile-template-auto-populate.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nSESSIONS_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\"\nPROFILES_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/profiles\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$SESSIONS_DIR\" \"$PROFILES_DIR\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no session log at all → exit 0; no card created ---\nclean_state\nRC=0\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" MEMORY_ETL_DISABLE=1 \\\n  bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || RC=$?\nif [ \"$RC\" != \"0\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected exit 0;
